## Original DNA event

Origin status: `catalogued_allele; original_DNA_VAF_metadata`.

Original GRCh38 VCF-style allele: **chr6:26055824** `ACCTTGGGCTTAGCGG` → `A`.

Source consequence: `inframe_deletion`; source protein label: `p.Ala197_Lys201del`; source transcript: `NM_005319`; cDNA label: `c.590_604del`.

After removing VCF padding: genomic **[26055824,26055839)**; `CCTTGGGCTTAGCGG` → `∅`; net length change **-15 nt**. This is genomic placement, not HGVS right normalization or a demonstrated mature-RNA edit.

## RNA screen outcomes

Minus-strand local allele, if the affected exon is retained: `CCGCTAAGCCCAAGG` → `∅`. Coding windows below establish local RNA sequence in the stated products; they do not establish the full mature transcript. An intronic or exon-boundary event can alter splicing; its mature-RNA outcome remains unresolved without an observed altered splice path. The genomic length change applies to a coding frame only where that literal edit is retained in CDS; intronic bases are not automatically translated.
